| HazardousItem | MedicalFirstAidGuideCode | 0..1 | A code signifying a medical first aid guide appropriate to this hazardous item. |

| HazardousItem | PlacardNotation | 0..1 | Text of the placard notation corresponding to the hazard class of this hazardous item. Can also be the hazard identification number of the orange placard (upper part) required on the means of transport. |

| HazardousItem | ProperShippingName | 0..1 | The proper shipping name supplemented. |
| HazardousItem | Quantity | 0..1 | The quantity of goods items in this hazardous item that are hazardous. |

| Parent | Element | Card. | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|
| AwardingTerms | AwardingCriterion | 0..n | Defines a criterion for awarding this tender. |
| AwardingTerms | BindingOnBuyerIndicator | 0..1 | Indicates if the decision is binding on the buyer (true) or not (false).  |
| AwardingTerms | Description | 0..n | Text describing terms under which the contract is to be awarded. |
| AwardingTerms | FollowupContractIndicator | 0..1 | Indicates if any service contract following the contest will be awarded to the winner or one of the winners of the contest (true) or not (false). |
| AwardingTerms | LowTendersDescription | 0..n | Text describing the exclusion criterion for abnormally low tenders. |
| AwardingTerms | NoFurtherNegotiationIndicator | 0..1 | Indicates if no further negotiation is allowed (true) or not (false).  |
| AwardingTerms | PaymentDescription | 0..n | Details of payments to all participants. |
| AwardingTerms | Prize | 0..n | Information about the value amount that will be offered to the winner depending on his rank. |
| AwardingTerms | PrizeDescription | 0..n | Number and value of the prizes to be awarded. |
| AwardingTerms | PrizeIndicator | 0..1 | Indicates whether a prize will be awarded (true) or not (false). |
| AwardingTerms | TechnicalCommitteeDescription | 0..n | Text describing the committee of experts evaluating the subjective criteria for awarding the contract. |
| AwardingTerms | TechnicalCommitteePerson | 0..n | A member of a committee of experts evaluating the subjective criteria for awarding the contract. |
| AwardingTerms | WeightingAlgorithmCode | 0..1 | A code signifying the weighting algorithm for awarding criteria. When multiple awarding criteria is used, different weighting and choices management algorithms based upon scores and weights of all award criteria can be used. An algorithm for weighting criteria will be reported in the call for tenders document. It is used to determine how to perform the final management of tenders based on the results in each of the established award criteria |

</details>

<details><summary><b>UtilityStatement</b> — 204 elements</summary>

| Parent | Element | Card. | Definition |
|---|---|---|---|
| Consumption | AllowanceCharge | 0..n | An allowance or charges that may apply with this consumption. |
| Consumption | EnergyWaterSupply | 0..1 | The details of any energy or water consumption. |
| Consumption | LegalMonetaryTotal | 1 | The total amount payable on this consumption, including any allowances, charges, or taxes. |
| Consumption | MainPeriod | 0..1 | The period of consumption. |
| Consumption | TaxTotal | 0..n | The total of taxes for each tax type covering the consumption. |
| Consumption | TelecommunicationsSupply | 0..1 | The details of any telecommunications consumption. |
| Consumption | UtilityStatementTypeCode | 0..1 | A code identifying the type of the Utility Statement required for this consumption. Explains the kind of utility the statement is about, e.g.. "gas", "electricity", "telephone" |
| ConsumptionAverage | AverageAmount | 0..1 | The average monetary amount of the consumption. |
| ConsumptionAverage | Description | 0..n | A description of the average consumed. |

| ConsumptionCorrection | ConsumptionWaterQuantity | 0..1 | Your consumpt for district heating water. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | CorrectionAmount | 0..1 | Your correction for heating correction. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | CorrectionType | 0..1 | Statement for the correction type. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | CorrectionTypeCode | 0..1 | Statement at the code for the correction type. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | CorrectionUnitAmount | 0..1 | Correction per MWH per degree C. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | Description | 0..n | Description related to the corrections. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | DifferenceTemperatureReductionQuantity | 0..1 | Deviation from standard heating correction. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | GasPressureQuantity | 0..1 | Correction of the gas pressure. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | MeterNumber | 0..1 | Statement for meter number. |
| ConsumptionCorrection | NormalTemperatureReductionQuantity | 0..1 | Statement for the standard for heating correction temperature. |

| ConsumptionHistory | ConsumptionLevelCode | 0..1 | The consumption level, expressed as a code used explain the consumption quantity, e.g.. diversion from the normal. |
| ConsumptionHistory | Description | 0..n | Text describing the consumption itself. |
| ConsumptionHistory | MeterNumber | 0..1 | A text identifier for the meter measuring the consumption. |
| ConsumptionHistory | Period | 1 | The period during which the consumption took place. |
| ConsumptionHistory | Quantity | 1 | The quantity consumed. |
